## Narration 1552

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1571)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1571]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1571</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1571 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَأَلْتُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ كَمْ فِي إِصْبَعِ الْمَرْأَةِ فَقَالَ عَشْرٌ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ كَمْ فِي إِصْبَعَيْنِ قَالَ عِشْرُونَ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ كَمْ فِي ثَلاَثٍ فَقَالَ ثَلاَثُونَ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ كَمْ فِي أَرْبَعٍ قَالَ عِشْرُونَ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ حِينَ عَظُمَ جُرْحُهَا وَاشْتَدَّتْ مُصِيبَتُهَا نَقَصَ عَقْلُهَا فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ أَعِرَاقِيٌّ أَنْتَ فَقُلْتُ بَلْ عَالِمٌ مُتَثَبِّتٌ أَوْ جَاهِلٌ مُتَعَلِّمٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ هِيَ السُّنَّةُ يَا ابْنَ أَخِي ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي أَصَابِعِ الْكَفِّ إِذَا قُطِعَتْ فَقَدْ تَمَّ عَقْلُهَا وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ خَمْسَ الأَصَابِعِ إِذَا قُطِعَتْ كَانَ عَقْلُهَا عَقْلَ الْكَفِّ خَمْسِينَ مِنَ الإِبِلِ فِي كُلِّ إِصْبَعٍ عَشَرَةٌ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَحِسَابُ الأَصَابِعِ ثَلاَثَةٌ وَثَلاَثُونَ دِينَارٍ وَثُلُثُ دِينَارٍ فِي كُلِّ أَنْمُلَةٍ وَهِيَ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ثَلاَثُ فَرَائِضَ وَثُلُثُ فَرِيضَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al Musayyab (successor). Select the question-and-answer exchange with Said ibn al-Musayyab. Preserve the incomplete opening narrator name as printed rather than filling it from another source. Malik&#x27;s following calculations remain commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman said, &quot;I asked Said ibn al Musayyab, &#x27;How much for the finger of a woman?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Ten camels&#x27; I said, &#x27;How much for two fingers?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Twenty camels.&#x27; I said, &#x27;How much for three?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Thirty camels.&#x27; I said, &#x27;How much for four?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Twenty camels.&#x27; I said, &#x27;When her wound is greater and her affliction stronger, is her blood-money then less?&#x27; He said, &#x27;Are you an Iraqi?&#x27; I said, &#x27;Rather, I am a scholar who seeks to verify things, or an ignorant man who seeks to learn.&#x27; Said said, &#x27;It is the sunna, my nephew.&#x27; &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1571]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1571</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1571 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community about all the fingers of the hand being cut off is that its blood- money is complete. That is because when five fingers are cut, their blood-money is the blood-money of the hand:fifty camels. Each finger has ten camels.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The reckoning of the fingers is thirty-three dinars for each fingertip, and that is three and a third shares of camels</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1571</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1571 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिया बिन अबी अब्दुर्रहमान कहते हैं मैंने सईद बिन मुसैय्यब से पूछा के औरत की उँगली में क्या है तो उन्होंने कहा के दस ऊँट हैं, मैंने कहा दो उँगलियों में तो उन्होंने कहा बीस ऊँट हैं, मैंने कहा तीन उँगलियों में तो उन्होंने कहा तीस ऊँट, मैंने कहा चार उँगलियों में तो उन्होंने कहा बीस ऊँट। मैंने कहा क्या ख़ूब! जब ज़ख़्म ज़्यादा हो गया और नुक़सान ज़्यादा हो तो दियत कम हो गई? सईद ने कहा क्या तू इराक़ी है? मैंने कहा नहीं, बल्कि मुझे जिस चीज़ का इल्म है उस पर जमा हुआ हूँ और जो चीज़ नहीं जानता उसको पूछता हूँ। सईद ने कहा के सुन्नत में ऐसा ही है ऐ मेरे भाई के बेटे। कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम इजमाली है के जब पूरी एक हथेली की उँगलियाँ काट डाली जाएँ तो दियत लाज़िम होगी इस हिसाब से के हर उँगली में दस ऊँट तो पचास ऊँट लाज़िम होंगे और हथेली भी अगर उसकी काटी जाए तो उसमें हाकिम की राय के मुवाफ़िक देना होगा। दनानीर के हिसाब से हर उँगली के सौ दीनार और हर एक पोर के तैंतीस दीनार हुए और हर एक पोर के तीन ऊँट और सुलुस ऊँट हुए।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ربیعہ بن ابی عبدالرحمن کہتے ہیں میں نے سعید بن مسیب سے پوچھا کہ عورت کی انگلی میں کیا ہے تو انہوں نے کہا کہ دس اونٹ ہیں میں نے کہا دو انگلیوں میں تو انہوں نے کہا بیس اونٹ ہیں میں نے کہا تین انگلیوں میں تو انہوں نے کہا تیس اونٹ میں نے کہا چار انگلیوں میں تو انہوں نے کہا بیس اونٹ میں نے کہا کیا خوب جب زخم زیادہ ہو گیا اور نقصان زیادہ ہو تو دیت کم ہوگئی سعد نے کہا کیا تو عراقی ہے میں نے کہا نہیں بلکہ مجھے جس چیز کا علم ہے اس پر جما ہوا ہوں اور جو چیز نہیں جانتا اس کو پوچھتا ہوں سعید نے کہا کہ سنت میں ایسا ہی ہے اے میرے بھائی کے بیٹے کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اجماعی ہے کہ جب پوری ایک ہتھیلی کی انگلیاں کاٹ ڈالی جائیں تو دیت لازم ہوگی اس حساب سے کہ ہر انگلی میں دس اونٹ تو پچاس اونٹ لازم ہوں گے اور ہتھیلی بھی اگر اس کی کاٹی جائے تو اس میں حاکم کی رائے کے موافق دینا ہو گا۔ دنانیر کے حساب سے ہر انگلی کے سو دینار اور ہر ایک پور کے تینتیس دینار ہوئے اور ہر ایک پور کے تین اونٹ اور ثلث اونٹ ہوئے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1571]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1571</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1571 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Rabia bin Abi Abdurrahman kehte hain maine Saeed bin Musayyib se poocha ke aurat ki ungli mein kya hai toh unhone kaha ke das oont hain, maine kaha do ungliyon mein toh unhone kaha bees oont hain, maine kaha teen ungliyon mein toh unhone kaha tees oont, maine kaha chaar ungliyon mein toh unhone kaha bees oont. Maine kaha kya khoob! Jab zakhm zyada ho gaya aur nuqsan zyada ho toh diyat kam ho gayi? Saeed ne kaha kya tu Iraqi hai? Maine kaha nahi, balki mujhe jis cheez ka ilm hai us par jama hua hoon aur jo cheez nahi jaanta usko poochta hoon. Saeed ne kaha ke sunnat mein aisa hi hai ae mere bhai ke bete. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek yeh hukum ijmali hai ke jab poori ek hatheli ki ungliyan kaat dali jayen toh diyat laazim hogi is hisaab se ke har ungli mein das oont toh pachaas oont laazim honge aur hatheli bhi agar uski kaati jaye toh usmein hakim ki raye ke muwafiq dena hoga. Dananeer ke hisaab se har ungli ke sau dinar aur har ek poor ke taintis dinar hue aur har ek poor ke teen oont aur sulus oont hue.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1571" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source reports Umar&#x27;s decision about the named injuries and amounts. Preserve this Companion attribution and exact source list.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Muslim ibn Jundub from Aslam, the mawla of Umar ibn al-Khattab that Umar ibn al-Khattab decided on a camel for a molar, a camel for a collar-bone, and a camel for a rib</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1572]</p>

## Narration 1554

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1573)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1573]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1573" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1573</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1573 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ قَضَى عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فِي الأَضْرَاسِ بِبَعِيرٍ بَعِيرٍ وَقَضَى مُعَاوِيَةُ بْنُ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ فِي الأَضْرَاسِ بِخَمْسَةِ أَبْعِرَةٍ خَمْسَةِ أَبْعِرَةٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ سَعِيدُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ فَالدِّيَةُ تَنْقُصُ فِي قَضَاءِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ وَتَزِيدُ فِي قَضَاءِ مُعَاوِيَةَ فَلَوْ كُنْتُ أَنَا لَجَعَلْتُ فِي الأَضْرَاسِ بَعِيرَيْنِ بَعِيرَيْنِ فَتِلْكَ الدِّيَةُ سَوَاءٌ وَكُلُّ مُجْتَهِدٍ مَأْجُورٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1573" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). Said ibn al-Musayyab compares the reported decisions of Umar and Muawiya and adds his own assessment. Keep the transmitted account with each source speaker&#x27;s own attribution; it is not Prophetic speech.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab decided on a camel for each molar, and Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan decided on five camels for each molar.&quot; Said ibn al-Musayyab said, &quot;The blood-money is less in the judgement of Umar ibn al-Khattab and more in the judgement of Muawiya. Had it been me, I would have made it two camels for each molar. That is the fair blood-money, and every one who strives with ijtihad is rewarded</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1573]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1573" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ إِذَا أُصِيبَتِ السِّنُّ فَاسْوَدَّتْ فَفِيهَا عَقْلُهَا تَامًّا فَإِنْ طُرِحَتْ بَعْدَ أَنْ تَسْوَدَّ فَفِيهَا عَقْلُهَا أَيْضًا تَامًّا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1574" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this short statement about a tooth to Said ibn al-Musayyab. Retain its source wording without supplying an external legal gloss.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab used to say,&#x27; &#x27;When a tooth is struck and becomes black, there is complete blood- money for it. If it falls out after it becomes black, there is also the complete blood-money for it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1574]</p>

## Narration 1556

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ دَاوُدَ بْنِ الْحُصَيْنِ، عَنْ أَبِي غَطَفَانَ بْنِ طَرِيفٍ الْمُرِّيِّ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ بَعَثَهُ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ يَسْأَلُهُ مَاذَا فِي الضِّرْسِ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ فِيهِ خَمْسٌ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَرَدَّنِي مَرْوَانُ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ فَقَالَ أَتَجْعَلُ مُقَدَّمَ الْفَمِ مِثْلَ الأَضْرَاسِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ لَوْ لَمْ تَعْتَبِرْ ذَلِكَ إِلاَّ بِالأَصَابِعِ عَقْلُهَا سَوَاءٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1575" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas answering Marwans two inquiries through Abu Ghatafan. The full field supplies the molar compensation answer, follow-up question and Ibn Abbas comparison with fingers.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Da&#x27;ud ibn al-Husayn that Abu Ghatafan ibn Tarif al-Murri informed him that Marwan ibn al-Hakam sent him to Abdullah ibn Abbas to ask him what there was for the molar. Abdullah ibn Abbas said, &quot;There are five camels for it.&quot; He said, &quot;Marwan sent me back again to Abdullah ibn Abbas.&#x27;&#x27; He said, &quot;Do you make front teeth like molars?&quot; Abdullah ibn Abbas said, &quot;It is enough that you take the fingers as the example for that, their blood-moneys being all the same</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1575]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1575" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अबी ग़तफ़ान बिन तरीफ़ से रिवायत है के मरवान बिन हकम ने उनको भेजा अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास यह पूछने को के दाढ़ में क्या दियत है? इब्न अब्बास ने कहा के पाँच ऊँट हैं। मरवान ने फिर उनको भेजा और कहलाया के क्या दाँत सामने के और दाढ़ें दियत में बराबर हैं? इब्न अब्बास ने कहा के अगर तू दाँतों को उँगलियों पर क़ियास कर लेता तो काफ़ी था, हर एक उँगली की दियत एक ही है। अगर मन्फ़अत किसी से कम है किसी से ज़्यादा, ऐसा ही दाँत और दाढ़ भी सब यकसाँ हैं।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1575" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abi Ghatfan bin Tarif se riwayat hai ke Marwan bin Hakam ne unko bheja Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas yeh poochne ko ke daarh mein kya diyat hai? Ibn Abbas ne kaha ke paanch oont hain. Marwan ne phir unko bheja aur kehlaaya ke kya daant saamne ke aur daarhein diyat mein barabar hain? Ibn Abbas ne kaha ke agar tu daanton ko ungliyon par qiyas kar leta toh kaafi tha, har ek ungli ki diyat ek hi hai. Agar manfaat kisi se kam hai kisi se zyada, aisa hi daant aur daarh bhi sab yaksan hain.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1575" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1557

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1576)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يُسَوِّي بَيْنَ الأَسْنَانِ فِي الْعَقْلِ وَلاَ يُفَضِّلُ بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ مُقَدَّمَ الْفَمِ وَالأَضْرَاسِ وَالأَنْيَابِ عَقْلُهَا سَوَاءٌ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ فِي السِّنِّ خَمْسٌ مِنَ الإِبِلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَالضِّرْسُ سِنٌّ مِنَ الأَسْنَانِ لاَ يَفْضُلُ بَعْضُهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1576</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1576 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father made all the teeth the same in the blood-money and did not prefer any kind over others. Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that the front teeth, molars, and eye-teeth have the same blood-money. That is because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The tooth has five camels.&#x27; The molar is one of the teeth and he did not prefer any kind over the others</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उरवाह बिन ज़ुबैर कहते थे के अगले ज़माने में सब दाँतों की दियत बराबर थी, कोई दूसरे पर ज़्यादा ना थी। कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम है के दाँत और कुचलियाँ और दाढ़ें सब बराबर हैं, क्योंकि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने हर दाँत में पाँच ऊँट का हुकुम किया, दाढ़ भी एक दाँत है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1576" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر کہتے تھے کہ اگلے زمانے میں سب دانتوں کی دیت برابر تھی کوئی دوسرے پر زیادہ نہ تھی ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم ہے کہ دانت اور کچلیاں اور داڑھیں سب برابر ہیں کیونکہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ہر دانت میں پانچ اونٹ کا حکم کیا داڑھ بھی ایک دانت ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1576]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair kehte the ke agle zamane mein sab daanton ki diyat barabar thi, koi dusre par zyada na thi. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek yeh hukum hai ke daant aur kuchliyan aur daarhein sab barabar hain, kyunke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne har daant mein paanch oont ka hukum kiya, daarh bhi ek daant hai.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، كَانَا يَقُولاَنِ فِي مُوضِحَةِ الْعَبْدِ نِصْفُ عُشْرِ ثَمَنِهِ ‏.‏</p>

## Narration 1559

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1578)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1578]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1578</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1578 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ، كَانَ يَقْضِي فِي الْعَبْدِ يُصَابُ بِالْجِرَاحِ أَنَّ عَلَى مَنْ جَرَحَهُ قَدْرَ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ثَمَنِ الْعَبْدِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ فِي مُوضِحَةِ الْعَبْدِ نِصْفَ عُشْرِ ثَمَنِهِ وَفِي مُنَقَّلَتِهِ الْعُشْرُ وَنِصْفُ الْعُشْرِ مِنْ ثَمَنِهِ وَفِي مَأْمُومَتِهِ وَجَائِفَتِهِ فِي كُلِّ وَاحِدَةٍ مِنْهُمَا ثُلُثُ ثَمَنِهِ وَفِيمَا سِوَى هَذِهِ الْخِصَالِ الأَرْبَعِ مِمَّا يُصَابُ بِهِ الْعَبْدُ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ثَمَنِهِ يُنْظَرُ فِي ذَلِكَ بَعْدَ مَا يَصِحُّ الْعَبْدُ وَيَبْرَأُ كَمْ بَيْنَ قِيمَةِ الْعَبْدِ بَعْدَ أَنْ أَصَابَهُ الْجُرْحُ وَقِيمَتِهِ صَحِيحًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يُصِيبَهُ هَذَا ثُمَّ يَغْرَمُ الَّذِي أَصَابَهُ مَا بَيْنَ الْقِيمَتَيْنِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْعَبْدِ إِذَا كُسِرَتْ يَدُهُ أَوْ رِجْلُهُ ثُمَّ صَحَّ كَسْرُهُ فَلَيْسَ عَلَى مَنْ أَصَابَهُ شَىْءٌ فَإِنْ أَصَابَ كَسْرَهُ ذَلِكَ نَقْصٌ أَوْ عَثَلٌ كَانَ عَلَى مَنْ أَصَابَهُ قَدْرُ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ثَمَنِ الْعَبْدِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الْقِصَاصِ بَيْنَ الْمَمَالِيكِ كَهَيْئَةِ قِصَاصِ الأَحْرَارِ نَفْسُ الأَمَةِ بِنَفْسِ الْعَبْدِ وَجُرْحُهَا بِجُرْحِهِ فَإِذَا قَتَلَ الْعَبْدُ عَبْدًا عَمْدًا خُيِّرَ سَيِّدُ الْعَبْدِ الْمَقْتُولِ فَإِنْ شَاءَ قَتَلَ وَإِنْ شَاءَ أَخَذَ الْعَقْلَ فَإِنْ أَخَذَ الْعَقْلَ أَخَذَ قِيمَةَ عَبْدِهِ وَإِنْ شَاءَ رَبُّ الْعَبْدِ الْقَاتِلِ أَنْ يُعْطِيَ ثَمَنَ الْعَبْدِ الْمَقْتُولِ فَعَلَ وَإِنْ شَاءَ أَسْلَمَ عَبْدَهُ فَإِذَا أَسْلَمَهُ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ غَيْرُ ذَلِكَ وَلَيْسَ لِرَبِّ الْعَبْدِ الْمَقْتُولِ إِذَا أَخَذَ الْعَبْدَ الْقَاتِلَ وَرَضِيَ بِهِ أَنْ يَقْتُلَهُ وَذَلِكَ فِي الْقِصَاصِ كُلِّهِ بَيْنَ الْعَبِيدِ فِي قَطْعِ الْيَدِ وَالرِّجْلِ وَأَشْبَاهِ ذَلِكَ بِمَنْزِلَتِهِ فِي الْقَتْلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الْعَبْدِ الْمُسْلِمِ يَجْرَحُ الْيَهُودِيَّ أَوِ النَّصْرَانِيَّ إِنَّ سَيِّدَ الْعَبْدِ إِنْ شَاءَ أَنْ يَعْقِلَ عَنْهُ مَا قَدْ أَصَابَ فَعَلَ أَوْ أَسْلَمَهُ فَيُبَاعُ فَيُعْطِي الْيَهُودِيَّ أَوِ النَّصْرَانِيَّ مِنْ ثَمَنِ الْعَبْدِ دِيَةَ جُرْحِهِ أَوْ ثَمَنَهُ كُلَّهُ إِنْ أَحَاطَ بِثَمَنِهِ وَلاَ يُعْطِي الْيَهُودِيَّ وَلاَ النَّصْرَانِيَّ عَبْدًا مُسْلِمًا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Marwan ibn al-Hakam (other). Select the reported decision of Marwan ibn al-Hakam. Malik&#x27;s subsequent detailed statement of community practice remains source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me that he had heard that Marwan ibn al-Hakam gave a decision about a slave who was injured that the person who injured him had to pay what he had diminished of the value of the slave.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1578]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1578</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1578 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that for the head wound of a slave that bares the bone, there is a twentieth of his price. The head wound which splinters the bone is three twentieths of his price. Both the wound to the brain and the belly wound are a third of his price. Besides these four, any other types of injury that decrease the price of the slave are considered after the slave is better and well, and one sees what the value of the slave is after his injury and what his value whole was before he had the injury. Then the one who injured him pays the difference between the two values</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सुलैमान बिन यसर कहते थे के घुलम के मौज़िहा में उसकी क़ीमत का बीसवां हिस्सा देना होगा. मरवान बिन हकम फ़ैसला करता था उस शख्स पर जो ज़ख़्मी करे घुलम को के जिस क़दर उस ज़ख्म की वजह से उसकी कीमत में नुक़सान हुआ वह ऐडा करे. कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम है के घुलम के मौज़िहा में उसकी कीमत का बीसवां हिस्सा और मणकाला में दसवां हिस्सा और बीसवां हिस्सा और मामूमह और जैफा में तीसरा हिस्सा देना होगा इनके और तरह के ज़ख़्मों में जिस क़दर क़ीमत में नुकसान हो गया देना होगा के उसकी क़ीमत इस ज़ख्म से पहले क्या थी और अब कितनी है. जिस क़दर कमी होगी वह देनी होगी. कहा मालिक ने जब घुलम का हाथ या पाँव कोई शख्स तोड़ डाले फिर वह अच्छा हो जाये तोह कुछ तवं नहीं होगा जाये तोह उसका तवं देना होगा. कहा मालिक ने ग़ुलामों में और लौंडियों में क़िसास का हुकुम मिस्ल अज़ादों के होगा घुलम लौंड़ी को क़सदन क़त्ल करे तोह घुलम भी क़त्ल किया जायेगा घुलम ने दूसरे घुलम को अमदन मार डाला तोह मक़तूल के मौला को इख़्तियार होगा चाहे क़ातिल को क़त्ल करे चाहे दियत यानि अपने घुलम की क़ीमत ले ले. क़ातिल के मौला को इख़्तियार है चाहे मक़तूल की क़ीमत अदा करे और क़ातिल को अपने पास रहने दे चाहे कतील ही को हवाले करदे हो कर क़ातिल को ले ले तोह फिर उसको क़त्ल न करे. इसी तरह अगर एक घुलम दूसरे घुलम का हाथ या पांव काटे तोह उसके क़िसास का भी यही हुकुम है. कहा मालिक ने अगर मुस्लमान घुलम किसी यहूदि या नसरानी को ज़ख़्मी करे तोह घुलम के मौला को इख़्तियार है चाहे दियत दे या घुलम को हवाले करदे तोह उस ग़ुलाम को बेच कर उसकी दियत ऐडा करेंगे वह घुलम यहुदी या नसरानी के पास रह नहीं सकता (क्युंके मुसलमान को काफ़िर का महकूम करना दुरूस्त नहीं).</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار کہتے تھے کہ غلام کے موضحہ میں اس کی قیمت کا بیسواں حصہ دینا ہوگا ۔ مروان بن حکم فیصلہ کرتا تھا اس شخص پر جو زخمی کرے غلام کو کہ جس قدر اس زخم کی وجہ سے اس کی قیمت میں نقصان ہوا وہ ادا کرے ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم ہے کہ غلام کے موضحہ میں اس کی قیمت کا بیسواں حصہ اور منقلہ میں دسواں حصہ اور بیسواں حصہ اور مامومہ اور جائفہ میں تیسرا حصہ دینا ہوگاسوائے ان کے اور طرح کے زخموں میں جس قدر قیمت میں نقصان ہو گیا دینا ہوگا جب وہ غلام اچھا ہوجائے تب دیکھیں گے کہ اس کی قیمت اس زخم سے پہلے کیا تھی اور اب کتنی ہے۔ جس قدر کمی ہوگی وہ دینی ہو گی۔ کہا مالک نے جب غلام کا ہاتھ یا پاؤں کوئی شخص توڑ ڈالے پھر وہ اچھا ہوجائے تو کچھ تاوان نہیں ہوگا البتہ اگر کسی قدر نقصان رہ جائے تو اس کا تاوان دینا ہوگا۔ کہا مالک نے غلاموں میں اور لونڈیوں میں قصاص کا حکم مثل آزادوں کے ہوگا اگر غلام لونڈی کو قصداً قتل کرے تو غلام بھی قتل کیا جائے گا اگر اس کو زخمی کرے وہ بھی زخمی کیا جائے گا ایک غلام نے دوسرے غلام کو عمداً مار ڈالا تو مقتول کے مولیٰ کو اختیار ہوگا چاہے قاتل کو قتل کرے چاہے دیت یعنی اپنے غلام کی قیمت لے لے ۔ قاتل کے مولیٰ کو اختیار ہے چاہے مقتول کی قیمت ادا کرے اور قاتل کو اپنے پاس رہنے دے چاہے قاتل ہی کو حوالے کردے اس سے زیادہ اور کچھ لازم نہ آئے گا۔ اب جب مقتول کا مولیٰ دیت پر راضی ہو کر قاتل کو لے لے تو پھر اس کو قتل نہ کرے ۔ اسی طرح اگر ایک غلام دوسرے غلام کا ہاتھ یا پاؤں کاٹے تو اس کے قصاص کا بھی یہی حکم ہے۔ کہا مالک نے اگر مسلمان غلام کسی یہودی یا نصرانی کو زخمی کرے تو غلام کے مولیٰ کو اختیار ہے چاہے دیت دے یا غلام کو حوالے کردے تو اس غلام کو بیچ کر اس کی دیت ادا کریں گے مگر وہ غلام یہودی یا نصرانی کے پاس رہ نہیں سکتا (کیونکہ مسلمان کو کافر کا محکوم کرنا درست نہیں )۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1578]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1578</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1578 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib aur Sulaiman bin Yasar kehte the ke ghulam ke mauziha mein uski qeemat ka beeswan hissa dena hoga. Marwan bin Hakam faisla karta tha us shakhs par jo zakhmi kare ghulam ko ke jis qadar us zakhm ki wajah se uski qeemat mein nuqsan hua woh ada kare. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek yeh hukum hai ke ghulam ke mauziha mein uski qeemat ka beeswan hissa aur manqala mein daswan hissa aur beeswan hissa aur mamoomah aur jaifa mein teesra hissa dena hoga, siwaye inke aur tarah ke zakhmon mein jis qadar qeemat mein nuqsan ho gaya dena hoga, jab woh ghulam achha ho jaye tab dekhenge ke uski qeemat is zakhm se pehle kya thi aur ab kitni hai. Jis qadar kami hogi woh deni hogi. Kaha Malik ne jab ghulam ka haath ya paon koi shakhs tod daale phir woh achha ho jaye toh kuch tawan nahi hoga, albata agar kisi qadar nuqsan reh jaye toh uska tawan dena hoga. Kaha Malik ne ghulamon mein aur laundiyon mein qisas ka hukum misl azadon ke hoga, agar ghulam laundi ko qasdan qatl kare toh ghulam bhi qatl kiya jayega, agar usko zakhmi kare woh bhi zakhmi kiya jayega. Ek ghulam ne dusre ghulam ko amdan maar dala toh maqtool ke maula ko ikhtiyar hoga chahe qatil ko qatl kare chahe diyat yani apne ghulam ki qeemat le le. Qatil ke maula ko ikhtiyar hai chahe maqtool ki qeemat ada kare aur qatil ko apne paas rehne de chahe qatil hi ko hawale karde, isse zyada aur kuch laazim na aayega. Ab jab maqtool ka maula diyat par raazi ho kar qatil ko le le toh phir usko qatl na kare. Isi tarah agar ek ghulam dusre ghulam ka haath ya paon kaate toh uske qisas ka bhi yahi hukum hai. Kaha Malik ne agar musalman ghulam kisi yahudi ya nasrani ko zakhmi kare toh ghulam ke maula ko ikhtiyar hai chahe diyat de ya ghulam ko hawale karde toh us ghulam ko bech kar uski diyat ada karenge, magar woh ghulam yahudi ya nasrani ke paas reh nahi sakta (kyunke musalman ko kafir ka mehkum karna durust nahi).</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1578" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1560

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1579]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1579</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، قَضَى أَنَّ دِيَةَ الْيَهُودِيِّ، أَوِ النَّصْرَانِيِّ - إِذَا قُتِلَ أَحَدُهُمَا - مِثْلُ نِصْفِ دِيَةِ الْحُرِّ الْمُسْلِمِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنْ لاَ يُقْتَلَ مُسْلِمٌ بِكَافِرٍ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَقْتُلَهُ مُسْلِمٌ قَتْلَ غِيْلَةٍ فَيُقْتَلُ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1579" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). Select the decision attributed to Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz. The following legal discussion by Malik is retained separately as commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he heard that Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz gave a decision that when a jew or christian was killed, his blood-money was half the blood-money of a free muslim.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1579]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1579" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community, is that a muslim is not killed for a kafir unless the muslim kills him by deceit. Then he is killed for it</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने कहा के यहूदी या नसरानी की दियत आज़ाद मुस्लमान की दियत से निस्फ़ है. कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक यह हुकुम है के कोई मुसलमान काफ़िर के बदले में क़त्ल न किया जायेगा दे कर मार डाले तोह क़त्ल किया जायेगा.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے کہا کہ یہودی یا نصرانی کی دیت آزاد مسلمان کی دیت سے نصف ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم ہے کہ کوئی مسلمان کافر کے بدلے میں قتل نہ کیا جائے گا مگر جب مسلمان فریب سے اس کو دھوکہ دے کر مار ڈالے تو قتل کیا جائے گا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1579" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1561

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، كَانَ يَقُولُ دِيَةُ الْمَجُوسِيِّ ثَمَانِي مِائَةِ دِرْهَمٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهُوَ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَجِرَاحُ الْيَهُودِيِّ وَالنَّصْرَانِيِّ وَالْمَجُوسِيِّ فِي دِيَاتِهِمْ عَلَى حِسَابِ جِرَاحِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ فِي دِيَاتِهِمُ الْمُوضِحَةُ نِصْفُ عُشْرِ دِيَتِهِ وَالْمَأْمُومَةُ ثُلُثُ دِيَتِهِ وَالْجَائِفَةُ ثُلُثُ دِيَتِهِ فَعَلَى حِسَابِ ذَلِكَ جِرَاحَاتُهُمْ كُلُّهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1580" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). Select Sulayman ibn Yasar&#x27;s transmitted statement. Malik&#x27;s endorsement and subsequent calculations remain in source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Sulayman ibn Yasar said, &quot;The blood-money of a magian is eight hundred dirhams.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1580]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1580" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1580</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1580 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;This is what is done in our community.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The blood-monies of the jew, christian, and magian in their injuries, is according to the injury of the muslims in their blood-moneys. The head wound is a twentieth of his full blood-money. The wound that opens the head is a third of his blood-money. The belly-wound is a third of his blood-money. All their injuries are according to this calculation</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1580" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father said, &quot;The tribe is not obliged to pay blood-money for intentional murder. They pay blood-money for accidental killing</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1581]</p>

## Narration 1563

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1582]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1582</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1582 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَضَتِ السُّنَّةُ أَنَّ الْعَاقِلَةَ لاَ تَحْمِلُ شَيْئًا مِنْ دِيَةِ الْعَمْدِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَشَاءُوا ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ ‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1582" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). Keep the statement attributed to Ibn Shihab and the separately framed abbreviated transmission from Yahya ibn Said as two report groups. Retain &#x27;the same as that&#x27; exactly without filling in words for the abbreviated witness. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Yahya ibn Said (successor). Keep the statement attributed to Ibn Shihab and the separately framed abbreviated transmission from Yahya ibn Said as two report groups. Retain &#x27;the same as that&#x27; exactly without filling in words for the abbreviated witness.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ibn Shihab said, &quot;The precedent of the sunna is that the tribe are not liable for any blood- money of an intentional killing unless they wish that.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said the same as that</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1582]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझसे मालिक से रिवायत किया के इब्न शिहाब ने कहा: सुन्नत का उसूल यह है के क़बीला जान बूझ कर किये गए क़त्ल की दियत का ज़िम्मेदार नहीं है जब तक कि वह खुद ऐसा न चाहें. याह्या ने मुझसे मालिक से और उन्होंने याह्या बिन साइड से भी यही रवायत की है.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے مالک سے روایت کیا کہ ابن شہاب نے کہا: سنت کا اصول یہ ہے کہ قبیلہ جان بوجھ کر کیے گئے قتل کی دیت کا ذمہ دار نہیں ہے جب تک کہ وہ خود ایسا نہ چاہیں۔ یحییٰ نے مجھ سے مالک سے اور انہوں نے یحییٰ بن سعید سے بھی یہی روایت کی ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhse Malik se riwayat kiya ke Ibn Shihab ne kaha: Sunnat ka usool yeh hai ke qabeela jaan boojh kar kiye gaye qatl ki diyat ka zimmedaar nahi hai jab tak ke woh khud aisa na chahein. Yahya ne mujhse Malik se aur unhone Yahya bin Saeed se bhi yahi riwayat ki hai.</p>

## Narration 1564

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1584)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1584]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1584" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1584</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1584 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَ مَالِكٌ إِنَّ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ قَالَ مَضَتِ السُّنَّةُ فِي قَتْلِ الْعَمْدِ حِينَ يَعْفُو أَوْلِيَاءُ الْمَقْتُولِ أَنَّ الدِّيَةَ تَكُونُ عَلَى الْقَاتِلِ فِي مَالِهِ خَاصَّةً إِلاَّ أَنْ تُعِينَهُ الْعَاقِلَةُ عَنْ طِيبِ نَفْسٍ مِنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الدِّيَةَ لاَ تَجِبُ عَلَى الْعَاقِلَةِ حَتَّى تَبْلُغَ الثُّلُثَ فَصَاعِدًا فَمَا بَلَغَ الثُّلُثَ فَهُوَ عَلَى الْعَاقِلَةِ وَمَا كَانَ دُونَ الثُّلُثِ فَهُوَ فِي مَالِ الْجَارِحِ خَاصَّةً ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا فِيمَنْ قُبِلَتْ مِنْهُ الدِّيَةُ فِي قَتْلِ الْعَمْدِ أَوْ فِي شَىْءٍ مِنَ الْجِرَاحِ الَّتِي فِيهَا الْقِصَاصُ أَنَّ عَقْلَ ذَلِكَ لاَ يَكُونُ عَلَى الْعَاقِلَةِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَشَاءُوا وَإِنَّمَا عَقْلُ ذَلِكَ فِي مَالِ الْقَاتِلِ أَوِ الْجَارِحِ خَاصَّةً إِنْ وُجِدَ لَهُ مَالٌ فَإِنْ لَمْ يُوجَدْ لَهُ مَالٌ كَانَ دَيْنًا عَلَيْهِ وَلَيْسَ عَلَى الْعَاقِلَةِ مِنْهُ شَىْءٌ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَشَاءُوا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ تَعْقِلُ الْعَاقِلَةُ أَحَدًا أَصَابَ نَفْسَهُ عَمْدًا أَوْ خَطَأً بِشَىْءٍ وَعَلَى ذَلِكَ رَأْىُ أَهْلِ الْفِقْهِ عِنْدَنَا وَلَمْ أَسْمَعْ أَنَّ أَحَدًا ضَمَّنَ الْعَاقِلَةَ مِنْ دِيَةِ الْعَمْدِ شَيْئًا وَمِمَّا يُعْرَفُ بِهِ ذَلِكَ أَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏فَمَنْ عُفِيَ لَهُ مِنْ أَخِيهِ شَىْءٌ فَاتِّبَاعٌ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ وَأَدَاءٌ إِلَيْهِ بِإِحْسَانٍ‏}‏ فَتَفْسِيرُ ذَلِكَ - فِيمَا نُرَى وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ - أَنَّهُ مَنْ أُعْطِيَ مِنْ أَخِيهِ شَىْءٌ مِنَ الْعَقْلِ فَلْيَتْبَعْهُ بِالْمَعْرُوفِ وَلْيُؤَدِّ إِلَيْهِ بِإِحْسَانٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الصَّبِيِّ الَّذِي لاَ مَالَ لَهُ وَالْمَرْأَةِ الَّتِي لاَ مَالَ لَهَا إِذَا جَنَى أَحَدُهُمَا جِنَايَةً دُونَ الثُّلُثِ إِنَّهُ ضَامِنٌ عَلَى الصَّبِيِّ وَالْمَرْأَةِ فِي مَالِهِمَا خَاصَّةً إِنْ كَانَ لَهُمَا مَالٌ أُخِذَ مِنْهُ وَإِلاَّ فَجِنَايَةُ كُلِّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمَا دَيْنٌ عَلَيْهِ لَيْسَ عَلَى الْعَاقِلَةِ مِنْهُ شَىْءٌ وَلاَ يُؤْخَذُ أَبُو الصَّبِيِّ بِعَقْلِ جِنَايَةِ الصَّبِيِّ وَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ أَنَّ الْعَبْدَ إِذَا قُتِلَ كَانَتْ فِيهِ الْقِيمَةُ يَوْمَ يُقْتَلُ وَلاَ تَحْمِلُ عَاقِلَةُ قَاتِلِهِ مِنْ قِيمَةِ الْعَبْدِ شَيْئًا قَلَّ أَوْ كَثُرَ وَإِنَّمَا ذَلِكَ عَلَى الَّذِي أَصَابَهُ فِي مَالِهِ خَاصَّةً بَالِغًا مَا بَلَغَ وَإِنْ كَانَتْ قِيمَةُ الْعَبْدِ الدِّيَةَ أَوْ أَكْثَرَ فَذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِ فِي مَالِهِ وَذَلِكَ لأَنَّ الْعَبْدَ سِلْعَةٌ مِنَ السِّلَعِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). Select the explicitly transmitted statement of Ibn Shihab at the start of the field. Malik&#x27;s following legal explanations and Quran citation remain outside this quotation.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said that Ibn Shihab said, &quot;The precedent of the sunna in the intentional murder is that when the relatives of the murdered person relinquish retaliation, the blood-money is owed by the murderer from his own property unless the tribe helps him with it willingly.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1584]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1584" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1584</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1584 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that the blood- money is not obliged against the tribe until it has reached a third of the full amount and upwards. Whatever reaches a third is against the tribe, and whatever is below a third, is against the property of the one who did the injury.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things about which there is no dispute among us, in the case of someone who has the blood-money accepted from him in intentional murder or in any injury in which there is retaliation, is that that blood-money is not due from the tribe unless they wish it. The blood-money for that is from the property of the murderer or the injurer if he has property. If he does not have any property, it is a debt against him, and none of it is owed by the tribe unless they wish.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The tribe does not pay blood-money to anyone who injures himself, intentionally or accidentally. This is the opinion of the people of fiqh in our community. I have not heard that anyone has made the tribe liable for any blood-money incurred by intentional acts. Part of what is well-known of that is that Allah, the Blessed, and the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;Whoever has something pardoned him by his brother, should follow it with what is accepted and pay it with good will&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 178) The commentary on that - in our view - and Allah knows best, is that whoever gives his brother something of the blood- money, should follow it with what is accepted and pay him with good will.&quot; Malik spoke about a child who had no property and a woman who had no property. He said, &quot;When one of them causes an injury below a third of the blood-money, it is taken on behalf of the child and woman from their personal property, if they have property from which it may be taken. If not, the injury which each of them has caused is a debt against them. The tribe does not have to pay any of it and the father of a child is not liable for the blood-money of an injury caused by the child and he is not responsible for it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute, is that when a slave is killed, the value for him is that of the day on which he was killed. The tribe of the murderer is not liable for any of the value of the slave, great or small. That is the responsibility of the one who struck him from his own personal property as far as it covers. If the value of the slave is the blood- money or more, that is against him in his property. That is because the slave is a certain type of goods</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1584" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने इब्न शिहाब से रिवायत किया के अमदुन क़त्ल में सुन्नत का तरीक़ा यह है के जब मक़तूल के वारिस दियत माफ़ कर दें तो क़ातिल पर उसके अपने माल से दियत लाज़िम होती है, इल्ला यह के उसका क़बीला अपनी ख़ुशी से उसकी मदद करे। इमाम मालिक ने कहा के हमारे हां तरीक़ा यह है के दियत क़बीले पर तब तक लाज़िम नहीं होती जब तक वो दियत के तिहाई तक न पहुंच जाए, जो तिहाई तक पहुंचे वो क़बीले पर है और जो तिहाई से कम हो वो मुजरिम के अपने माल पर है।</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے ابن شہاب سے روایت کیا کہ عمدی قتل میں سنت کا طریقہ یہ ہے کہ جب مقتول کے ورثاء دیت معاف کر دیں تو قاتل پر اس کے اپنے مال سے دیت لازم ہوتی ہے، الا یہ کہ اس کا قبیلہ اپنی خوشی سے اس کی مدد کرے۔ امام مالک نے کہا کہ ہمارے ہاں طریقہ یہ ہے کہ دیت قبیلے پر تب تک لازم نہیں ہوتی جب تک وہ دیت کے تہائی تک نہ پہنچ جائے، جو تہائی تک پہنچے وہ قبیلے پر ہے اور جو تہائی سے کم ہو وہ مجرم کے اپنے مال پر ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne Ibn Shihab se riwayat kiya ke amdun qatl mein sunnat ka tareeqa yeh hai ke jab maqtool ke waris diyat maaf kar dein to qatil par uske apne maal se diyat lazim hoti hai, illa yeh ke uska qabeela apni khushi se uski madad kare. Imam Malik ne kaha ke hamare haan tareeqa yeh hai ke diyat qabeele par tab tak lazim nahi hoti jab tak woh diyat ke tihai tak na pahunch jaye, jo tihai tak pahunche woh qabeele par hai aur jo tihai se kam ho woh mujrim ke apne maal par hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1584" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1565

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1585)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1585]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1585" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1585</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1585 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، نَشَدَ النَّاسَ بِمِنًى مَنْ كَانَ عِنْدَهُ عِلْمٌ مِنَ الدِّيَةِ أَنْ يُخْبِرَنِي ‏.‏ فَقَامَ الضَّحَّاكُ بْنُ سُفْيَانَ الْكِلاَبِيُّ فَقَالَ كَتَبَ إِلَىَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ أُوَرِّثَ امْرَأَةَ أَشْيَمَ الضِّبَابِيِّ مِنْ دِيَةِ زَوْجِهَا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ ادْخُلِ الْخِبَاءَ حَتَّى آتِيَكَ فَلَمَّا نَزَلَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَخْبَرَهُ الضَّحَّاكُ فَقَضَى بِذَلِكَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ وَكَانَ قَتْلُ أَشْيَمَ خَطَأً ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1585" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar and ad-Dahhak ibn Sufyan in the supplied inquiry about blood-money inheritance. Select the actual inquiry, ad-Dahhaks report of the Messengers letter and Umars resulting decision. Ibn Shihabs final explanatory characterization of the killing remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that Umar ibn al- Khattab demanded of the people at Mina, &quot;If anyone has knowledge of blood-money, let him inform me.&quot; Ad-Dahhak ibn Sufyan al-Kilabi stood up and said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, wrote to me that the wife of Ashyam ad-Dibabi inherited from the blood-money of her husband.&quot; Umar ibn al-Khattab said to him, &quot;Go into the tent until I come to you.&quot; When Umar ibn al-Khattab came in, ad-Dahhak told him about it and Umar ibn al-Khattab gave a decision based on that. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1585]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1585" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">इब्न-इ-शिहाब से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने लोगों को मीणा में बुलाया और कहा कि जिस शख्स को दियत का मसला मालूम हो वो मुझ से बयां करे. तब ज़ाहहक बिन सुफ़यान किलबी खड़े हुए और कहा कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मुझे लिख भेजा था के अश्याम धीबाबी की औरत को मीरास दिलाओ अश्याम की दियत में से. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा तुम खेमे में जाओ जब तक मैं न आऊं. जब हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) आये तो ज़ाहहक ने यही बयां किया से मारा गया था.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے بلایا لوگوں کو منی میں اور کہا کہ جس شخص کو دیت کا مسئلہ معلوم ہو وہ بیان کرے مجھ سے، تو ضحاک بن سفیان کلابی کھڑے ہوئے اور کہا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے مجھے لکھ بھیجا تھا کہ اشیم ضبابی کی عورت کو میراث دلاؤں اشیم کی دیت میں سے حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا تو خیمے میں جا جب تک میں آؤں جب تک حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ آئے تو ضحاک نے یہی بیان کیا حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے اسی کا حکم کیا ابن شہاب نے کہ اشیم خطا سے مارا گیا تھا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn-e-Shihab se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne logon ko Mina mein bulaya aur kaha ke jis shakhs ko diyat ka masla maloom ho wo mujh se bayan kare. Tab Zahhak bin Sufyan Kilabi khade hue aur kaha ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mujhe likh bheja tha ke Ashyam Dhibabi ki aurat ko miras dilao Ashyam ki diyat mein se. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha tum khaime mein jao jab tak main na aaun. Jab Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) aaye to Zahhak ne yahi bayan kiya, Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne usi ka hukum diya. Ibn-e-Shihab ne kaha ke Ashyam khata se mara gaya tha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1585" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1566

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1586)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ شُعَيْبٍ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنْ بَنِي مُدْلِجٍ - يُقَالُ لَهُ قَتَادَةُ - حَذَفَ ابْنَهُ بِالسَّيْفِ فَأَصَابَ سَاقَهُ فَنُزِيَ فِي جُرْحِهِ فَمَاتَ فَقَدِمَ سُرَاقَةُ بْنُ جُعْشُمٍ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ لَهُ عُمَرُ اعْدُدْ عَلَى مَاءِ قُدَيْدٍ عِشْرِينَ وَمِائَةَ بَعِيرٍ حَتَّى أَقْدَمَ عَلَيْكَ فَلَمَّا قَدِمَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَخَذَ مِنْ تِلْكَ الإِبِلِ ثَلاَثِينَ حِقَّةً وَثَلاَثِينَ جَذَعَةً وَأَرْبَعِينَ خَلِفَةً ثُمَّ قَالَ أَيْنَ أَخُو الْمَقْتُولِ قَالَ هَا أَنَا ذَا ‏.‏ قَالَ خُذْهَا فَإِنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لَيْسَ لِقَاتِلٍ شَىْءٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Amr ibn Shuayb that a man of the Banu Mudlij called Qatada threw a sword at his son and it struck his thigh. The wound bled profusely and he died. Suraqa ibn Jusham came to Umar ibn al-Khattab and mentioned that to him Umar said to him, &quot;At the watering place of Qudayd count one hundred and twenty camels and wait until I come to you.&quot; When Umar ibn al-Khattab came to him, he took thirty four-year-old camels, thirty five-year-old camels, and forty pregnant camels from them. Then he said, &quot;Where is the brother of the slain man?&quot; He said, &quot;Here.&quot; He said, &quot;Take them. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;The killer gets nothing</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन शुऐब से रिवायत है के बनी मुदलज में से एक शख़्स जिसका नाम क़तादा था, उसने अपने लड़के को तलवार मारी जो उसकी पिंडली में लगी, ख़ून बंद न हुआ और आख़िर मर गया। फिर सुराक़ा बिन जुशम हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आए और उन से बयान किया। हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा क़दीद के पास 120 ऊंट तैयार रख जब तक मैं वहां आऊं। जब हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) वहां आए तो उन ऊंटों में से तीन हिक्क़े, बीस जज़ाए और चालीस हिक्क़े लिए। फिर कहा कौन है मक़तूल का भाई? उसने कहा क्यों, मैं मौजूद हूं न! कहा तो ये सब ऊंट ले ले, इस वास्ते के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया क़ातिल को मीरास नहीं मिलती। सईद बिन मुसय्यब और सुलईमान बिन यासार से सवाल हुआ के माह-ए-हराम में अगर कोई क़त्ल करे तो दियत में सख़्ती करेंगे? उन्होंने कहा नहीं, बल्कि बढ़ा देंगे बज़ह इन महीनों की हुरमत के। फिर सईद से पूछा गया के अगर कोई ज़ख़्मी करे इन महीनों में तो उसकी भी दियत बढ़ा देंगे जैसे क़त्ल की दियत बढ़ा देंगे? सईद ने कहा हां।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عمرو بن شعیب سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص نے بنی مدلج میں سے جس کا نام قتادہ تھا اپنے لڑکے کو تلوار ماری وہ اس کے پنڈلی میں لگی خون بند نہ ہوا آخر مر گیا تو سراقہ بن جعشم حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس آئے اور ان سے بیان کیا حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا قدید کے پاس ایک سو بیس اونٹ تیار رکھ جب تک میں وہاں آؤں جب حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ وہاں آئے تو ان اونٹوں میں سے تین حقے اور بیس جزعے لئے اور چالیس حقے لئے پھر کہا کون ہے مقتول کا بھائی؟ اس نے کہا کیوں میں موجود ہوں نا! کہا تو یہ سب اونٹ لے لے اس واسطے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا قاتل کو میراث نہیں ملتی سعید بن مسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار سے سوال ہوا کہ ماہ حرام میں اگر کوئی قتل کرے تو دیت میں سختی کریں گے انہوں نے کہا نہیں بلکہ بڑھا دیں گے بوجہ ان مہینوں کی حرمت کے پھر سعید سے پوچھا کہ اگر کوئی زخمی کرے ان مہینوں میں تو اس کی بھی دیت بڑھا دیں گے جیسسے قتل کی دیت بڑھا دیں گے سعید نے کہا ہاں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Amr bin Shuaib se riwayat hai ke Bani Mudlij mein se ek shakhs jiska naam Qatada tha, usne apne ladke ko talwar maari jo uski pindli mein lagi, khoon band na hua aur aakhir mar gaya. Phir Suraqa bin Jusham Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaye aur un se bayan kiya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha Qadeed ke paas 120 oont taiyar rakh jab tak main wahan aaun. Jab Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) wahan aaye to un oonton mein se teen hiqqe, bees jaza&#x27;e aur chalees hiqqe liye. Phir kaha kaun hai maqtool ka bhai? Usne kaha kyun, main mojood hoon na! Kaha to ye sab oont le le, is waaste ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya qatil ko miras nahi milti. Saeed bin Musayyib aur Sulaiman bin Yasar se sawal hua ke mah-e-haram mein agar koi qatl kare to diyat mein sakhti karenge? Unhone kaha nahi, balkeh badha denge bajah in mahino ki hurmat ke. Phir Saeed se poocha gaya ke agar koi zakhmi kare in mahino mein to uski bhi diyat badha denge jaise qatl ki diyat badha denge? Saeed ne kaha haan.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1586]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1586" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1567

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1587)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1587]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، سُئِلاَ أَتُغَلَّظُ الدِّيَةُ فِي الشَّهْرِ الْحَرَامِ فَقَالاَ لاَ وَلَكِنْ يُزَادُ فِيهَا لِلْحُرْمَةِ ‏.‏ فَقِيلَ لِسَعِيدٍ هَلْ يُزَادُ فِي الْجِرَاحِ كَمَا يُزَادُ فِي النَّفْسِ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ أُرَاهُمَا أَرَادَا مِثْلَ الَّذِي صَنَعَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ فِي عَقْلِ الْمُدْلِجِيِّ حِينَ أَصَابَ ابْنَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1587" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). Select the questions and responses attributed to Said and Sulayman. Malik&#x27;s subsequent interpretation and the incomplete parenthetical numerical explanation remain source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab and Sulayman ibn Yasar were asked, &quot;Does one deal more harshly in taking the blood-money in the sacred month?&quot; They said, &quot;No. But it is increased in it because of violating the month.&quot; It was said to Said, &quot;Does one increase for the wound as one increases for the life?&quot; He said, &quot;Yes.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1587]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1587" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1587</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1587 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik added, &quot;I think that they meant the same as what Umar ibn al-Khattab did with respect to the blood-money of the Mudliji when he struck his son.&quot; (i.e. giving 120 camels instead of)</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1587" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने कहा के उन्होंने सुना के सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब और सुलयमान इब्न यासार से पूछा गया, क्या हुरमत वाले महीने में दियत लेने में ज़्यादा सख़्ती की जाती है? उन्होंने कहा, नहीं, लेकिन महीने की बे-हुरमती की वजह से दियत में इज़ाफ़ा किया जाता है।</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne kaha ke unhone suna ke Said ibn al-Musayyab aur Sulayman ibn Yasar se poocha gaya, kya hurmat wale mahine mein diyat lene mein zyada sakhti ki jati hai? Unhone kaha, nahi, lekin mahine ki be-hurmati ki wajah se diyat mein izafa kiya jata hai.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّ رَجُلاً، مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ - يُقَالُ لَهُ أُحَيْحَةُ بْنُ الْجُلاَحِ - كَانَ لَهُ عَمٌّ صَغِيرٌ هُوَ أَصْغَرُ مِنْ أُحَيْحَةَ وَكَانَ عِنْدَ أَخْوَالِهِ فَأَخَذَهُ أُحَيْحَةُ فَقَتَلَهُ فَقَالَ أَخْوَالُهُ كُنَّا أَهْلَ ثُمِّهِ وَرُمِّهِ حَتَّى إِذَا اسْتَوَى عَلَى عُمَمِهِ غَلَبَنَا حَقُّ امْرِئٍ فِي عَمِّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ عُرْوَةُ فَلِذَلِكَ لاَ يَرِثُ قَاتِلٌ مَنْ قَتَلَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ قَاتِلَ الْعَمْدِ لاَ يَرِثُ مِنْ دِيَةِ مَنْ قَتَلَ شَيْئًا وَلاَ مِنْ مَالِهِ وَلاَ يَحْجُبُ أَحَدًا وَقَعَ لَهُ مِيرَاثٌ وَأَنَّ الَّذِي يَقْتُلُ خَطَأً لاَ يَرِثُ مِنَ الدِّيَةِ شَيْئًا وَقَدِ اخْتُلِفَ فِي أَنْ يَرِثَ مِنْ مَالِهِ لأَنَّهُ لاَ يُتَّهَمُ عَلَى أَنَّهُ قَتَلَهُ لِيَرِثَهُ وَلِيَأْخُذَ مَالَهُ فَأَحَبُّ إِلَىَّ أَنْ يَرِثَ مِنْ مَالِهِ وَلاَ يَرِثُ مِنْ دِيَتِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1588" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Urwa ibn az- Zubayr (successor). Retain Urwa&#x27;s narrated account and his stated conclusion together. Malik&#x27;s later general legal discussion is kept outside the report.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik related to me from Yahya ibn Said from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr that a man of the Ansar called Uhayha ibn al-Julah had a young paternal uncle who was younger than him and who was living with his maternal uncles. Uhayha took him and killed him. His maternal uncles said, &quot;We brought him up from a baby to a youth till he stood firm on his feet, and we have had the right of a man taken from us by his paternal uncle.&quot; Urwa said, &quot;For that reason a killer does not inherit from the one he killed.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1588]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1588" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1588</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1588 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things about which there is no dispute is that the intentional murderer does not inherit anything of the blood-money of the person he has murdered or any of his property. He does not stop anyone who has a share of inheritance from inheriting. The one who kills accidentally does not inherit anything of the blood-money and there is dispute as to whether or not he inherits from the dead person&#x27;s property because there is no suspicion that he killed him for his inheritance and in order to take his property. I prefer that he inherit from the dead person&#x27;s property and not inherit from the blood-money</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1588" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उर्वह बिन जुबैर से रिवायत है के अंसार का एक शख्स जिसका नाम ऐहैया बिन जुलाह था, उसका छोटा चाचा था जो अपनी ननिहाल में था। उसे ऐहैया ने ले कर मार डाला। उसकी ननिहाल के लोगों ने कहा हम ने पाला पोसा, जब जवान हुआ तो उसका भतीजा हम पर ग़ालिब आया और उसी ने ले लिया। उर्वह ने कहा इस वजह से कातिल मकतूल का वारिस नहीं होता। मालिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक इस में कुछ इख़्तिलाफ़ नहीं है के क़त्ल-ए-अम्द करने वाला मकतूल की दियत का वारिस नहीं होता, न उसके माल का, न वो किसी वारिस को महरूम कर सकता है। और क़त्ल-ए-ख़ता करने वाला दियत का वारिस नहीं होता लेकिन और माल का वारिस होता है या नहीं इस में इख़्तिलाफ़ है, मेरे नज़दीक वो और माल का वारिस होगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1588" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن زبیر سے روایت ہے کہ ایک شخص انصار کا جس کا نام احیحہ بن جلاح تھا اس سے چھوٹا چچا تھا وہ اپنی ننہیال میں تھا اس کو احیحہ نے لے کر مار ڈالا اس کے ننہیال کے لوگوں نے کہا ہم نے پالا پرورش کیا جب جوان ہوا تو اس کا بھتیجا ہم پر غالب آیا اور اسی نے لے لیا عروہ نے کہا اس وجہ سے قاتل مقتول کا وارث نہیں ہوتا ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک اس میں کچھ اختلاف نہیں ہے کہ قتل عمد کرنے والا مقتول کی دیت کا وارث نہیں ہوتا نہ اس کے مال کا نہ وہ کسی وارث کو محروم کرسکتا ہے اور قتل خطا کرنے والادیت کا وارث نہیں ہوتا لیکن اور مال کا وارث ہوتا ہے یا نہیں اس میں اختلاف ہے میرے نزدیک اور مال کا وارث ہو گا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Zubair se riwayat hai ke Ansar ka ek shakhs jiska naam Aihaiha bin Julah tha, uska chota chacha tha jo apni nanihyal mein tha. Usay Aihaiha ne le kar maar dala. Uski nanihyal ke logon ne kaha hum ne pala posa, jab jawan hua to uska bhatija hum par ghalib aaya aur usi ne le liya. Urwah ne kaha is wajah se qatil maqtool ka waris nahi hota. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek is mein kuch ikhtilaf nahi hai ke qatl-e-amd karne wala maqtool ki diyat ka waris nahi hota, na uske maal ka, na wo kisi waris ko mehroom kar sakta hai. Aur qatl-e-khata karne wala diyat ka waris nahi hota lekin aur maal ka waris hota hai ya nahi is mein ikhtilaf hai, mere nazdeek wo aur maal ka waris hoga.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1588" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1569

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1589)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَأَبِي، سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ جَرْحُ الْعَجْمَاءِ جُبَارٌ وَالْبِئْرُ جُبَارٌ وَالْمَعْدِنُ جُبَارٌ وَفِي الرِّكَازِ الْخُمُسُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1589</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1589 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab and Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The wound of an animal is of no account and no compensation is due for it. The well is of no account and no compensation is due for it. The mine is of no account and no compensation is due for it and a fifth is due for buried treasures.&quot; (Al-kanz:see Book 17). Malik said, &quot;Everyone leading an animal by the halter, driving it, and riding it is responsible for what the animal strikes unless the animal kicks out without anything being done to it to make it kick out. Umar ibn al-Khattab imposed the blood-money on a person who was exercising his horse.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is more fitting that a person leading an animal by the halter, driving it, or riding it incur a loss than a person who is exercising his horse.&quot; (See hadith 4 of this book). Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community about a person who digs a well on a road or ties up an animal or does the like of that on a road used by muslims, is that since what he has done is included in that which he is not permitted to do in such a place, he is liable for whatever injury or other thing arises from that action. The blood-money of that which is less than a third of the full blood- money is owed from his own personal property. Whatever reaches a third or more, is owed by his tribe. Any such things that he does which he is permitted to do on the muslims&#x27; road are something for which he has no liability or loss. Part of that is a hole which a man digs to collect rain, and the beast from which the man alights for some need and leaves standing on the road. There is no penalty against anyone for this.&quot; Malik spoke about a man who went down a well, and another man followed behind him, and the lower one pulled the higher one and they fell into the well and both died He said, &quot;The tribe of the one who pulled him in is responsible for the blood-money.&quot; Malik spoke about a child whom a man ordered to go down into a well or to climb a palm tree and he died as a result. He said, &quot;The one who ordered him is liable for whatever befalls him, be it death or something else.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute is that women and children are not obliged to pay blood-money together with the tribe in the blood-moneys which the tribe must pay. The blood-money is only obligatory for a man who has reached puberty.&quot; Malik said that the tribe could bind themselves to the blood-money of mawali if they wished. If they refused, they were people of the diwan or were cut off from their people. In the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, people paid the blood-money to each other as well as in the time of Abu Bakr as-Siddiq before there was a diwan. The diwan was in the time of Umar ibn al-Khattab. No one other than one&#x27;s people and the ones holding the wala&#x27; paid blood- money for one because the wala&#x27; was not transferable and because the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;The wala&#x27; belongs to the one who sets free.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The wala&#x27; is an established relationship.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community about animals that are injured is that the person who causes the injury pays whatever of their value has been diminished.&quot; Malik said about a man condemned to death and one of the other hudud befell him, &quot;He is not punished for it. That is because the killing overrides all of that, except for slander. The slander remains hanging over the one to whom it was said because it will be said to him, &#x27;Why do you not flog the one who slandered you?&#x27; I think that the condemned man is flogged with the hadd before he is killed, and then he is killed. I do not think that any retaliation is inflicted on him for any injury except killing because killing overrides all of that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that when a murdered person is found among the main body of a people in a village or other place, the house or place of the nearest people to him is not responsible. That is because the murdered person can be slain and then cast at the door of some people to shame them by it. No one is responsible for the like of that.&quot; Malik said about a group of people who fight with each other and when the fight is broken up, a man is found dead or wounded, and it is not known who did it, &quot;The best of what is heard about that is that there is blood-money for him, and the blood-money is against the people who argued with him. If the injured or slain person is not from either of the two parties, his blood-money is against both of the two parties together</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मालिक से, उन्होंने इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसैयब और अबू सलमा इब्न अब्द अर-रहमान से और उन्होंने अबू हुरयरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत किया के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जानवर का ज़ख्म रायगां है और उसकी कोई दियत नहीं, कुंआ रायगां है, कान रायगां है और दफीना में पांचवां हिस्सा है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مالک سے، انہوں نے ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن مسیب اور ابوسلمہ بن عبدالرحمٰن سے اور انہوں نے ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ سے روایت کیا کہ رسول اللہ (صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم) نے فرمایا: جانور کا زخم رائیگاں ہے اور اس کی کوئی دیت نہیں، کنواں رائیگاں ہے، کان رائیگاں ہے اور دفینہ میں پانچواں حصہ ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1589]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1589" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne Malik se, unhone Ibn Shihab se, unhone Said ibn al-Musayyab aur Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman se aur unhone Abu Hurayra (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat kiya ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Janwar ka zakhm rayegan hai aur uski koi diyat nahi, kunwa rayegan hai, kaan rayegan hai aur dafeena mein paanchwa hissa hai.</p>

## Narration 1570
