<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَتَلَ نَفَرًا خَمْسَةً أَوْ سَبْعَةً بِرَجُلٍ وَاحِدٍ قَتَلُوهُ قَتْلَ غِيلَةٍ وَقَالَ عُمَرُ لَوْ تَمَالأَ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلُ صَنْعَاءَ لَقَتَلْتُهُمْ جَمِيعًا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source reports Umar&#x27;s action and statement. Keep the source&#x27;s unresolved count &#x27;five or seven&#x27; rather than selecting or normalizing one number.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Said ibn al-Musayyab that Umar ibn al-Khattab killed five or seven people for one man whom they had killed secretly by trickery. Umar said, &quot;Had all the people of Sana joined forces against him, I would have killed them all</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1590]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1590" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिवायत है के हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने पांच या सात आदमियों को एक शख्स के बदले में क़त्ल किया. उन्होंने धोखा दे कर उसे मार डाला था. फिर हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा के अगर सारे सना&#x27;आ वाले उसके क़त्ल में शरीक होते तो मैं सब को क़त्ल करता.</p>

## Narration 1571

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1591)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1591]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1591" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1591</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1591 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ سَعْدِ بْنِ زُرَارَةَ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ حَفْصَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَتَلَتْ جَارِيَةً لَهَا سَحَرَتْهَا وَقَدْ كَانَتْ دَبَّرَتْهَا فَأَمَرَتْ بِهَا فَقُتِلَتْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ السَّاحِرُ الَّذِي يَعْمَلُ السِّحْرَ وَلَمْ يَعْمَلْ ذَلِكَ لَهُ غَيْرُهُ هُوَ مَثَلُ الَّذِي قَالَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَلَقَدْ عَلِمُوا لَمَنِ اشْتَرَاهُ مَا لَهُ فِي الآخِرَةِ مِنْ خَلاَقٍ‏}‏ فَأَرَى أَنْ يُقْتَلَ ذَلِكَ إِذَا عَمِلَ ذَلِكَ هُوَ نَفْسُهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1591" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Hafsa (companion). Select the source-attributed account of Hafsa&#x27;s action. Her relationship to the Prophet does not turn the action into Prophetic speech; Malik&#x27;s later interpretation and Quran citation remain commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Sad ibn Zurara that he had heard that Hafsa, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, killed one of her slave-girls who had used sorcery against her. She was a mudabbara. Hafsa gave the order, and she was killed.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1591]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1591" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The sorcerer is the one who uses sorcery for himself and no one else uses that for him. It is like the one about whom Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;They know the one who devotes himself to it will have no share in the Next World.&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 102) I think that that person is killed if he does that himself</p>

## Narration 1572

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1592)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1592]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1592</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1592 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ حُسَيْنٍ، مَوْلَى عَائِشَةَ بِنْتِ قُدَامَةَ أَنَّ عَبْدَ الْمَلِكِ بْنَ مَرْوَانَ، أَقَادَ وَلِيَّ رَجُلٍ مِنْ رَجُلٍ قَتَلَهُ بِعَصًا فَقَتَلَهُ وَلِيُّهُ بِعَصًا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ الرَّجُلَ إِذَا ضَرَبَ الرَّجُلَ بِعَصًا أَوْ رَمَاهُ بِحَجَرٍ أَوْ ضَرَبَهُ عَمْدًا فَمَاتَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ هُوَ الْعَمْدُ وَفِيهِ الْقِصَاصُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَقَتْلُ الْعَمْدِ عِنْدَنَا أَنْ يَعْمِدَ الرَّجُلُ إِلَى الرَّجُلِ فَيَضْرِبَهُ حَتَّى تَفِيظَ نَفْسُهُ وَمِنَ الْعَمْدِ أَيْضًا أَنْ يَضْرِبَ الرَّجُلُ الرَّجُلَ فِي النَّائِرَةِ تَكُونُ بَيْنَهُمَا ثُمَّ يَنْصَرِفُ عَنْهُ وَهُوَ حَىٌّ فَيُنْزَى فِي ضَرْبِهِ فَيَمُوتُ فَتَكُونُ فِي ذَلِكَ الْقَسَامَةُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ يُقْتَلُ فِي الْعَمْدِ الرِّجَالُ الأَحْرَارُ بِالرَّجُلِ الْحُرِّ الْوَاحِدِ وَالنِّسَاءُ بِالْمَرْأَةِ كَذَلِكَ وَالْعَبِيدُ بِالْعَبْدِ كَذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan (other). Select the historical decision attributed to Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan. Malik&#x27;s ensuing general discussion remains source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Umar ibn Husayn, the mawla of A&#x27;isha bint Qudama, that Abd al-Malik ibn Marwan imposed retaliation against a man who killed a mawla with a stick and so the mawla&#x27;s patron killed the man with a stick.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1592]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1592</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1592 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things in our community about which there is no dispute is that when a man strikes another man with a stick or hits him with a rock or intentionally strikes him causing his death, that is an intentional injury and there is retaliation for it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Intentional murder with us is that a man intentionally goes to a man and strikes him until his life goes. Part of intentional injury also is that a man strikes a man in a quarrel between them. He leaves him while he is alive, and he bleeds to death and so dies. There is retaliation for that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What is done in our community is that a group of free men are killed for the intentional murder of one free man, and a group of women for one woman, and a group of slaves for one slave</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1592]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1592</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1592 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मालिक ने कहा एक शख्स ने दूसरे को लकड़ी से मार डाला, अब्दुल मालिक बिन मरवान ने कातिल को वली-ए-मकतूल के हवाले किया, उसने भी उसको लकड़ी से मार डाला। मालिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक यह हुक्म इत्तेफ़ाक़ी है की अगर कोई शख्स किसी को लकड़ी या पत्थर से क़स्दन मारे और वो हलाक हो जाए तो क़िसास लिया जाएगा। मालिक ने कहा हमारे नज़दीक क़त्ल-ए-अम्द यही है की एक आदमी दूसरे को क़स्दन मारे यहां तक की उसका दम निकल जाए, और यह भी क़त्ल-ए-अम्द है की एक शख्स से दुश्मनी हो, उसको एक ज़र्ब लगा कर चला आए, उस वक़्त वो ज़िंदा हो, बाद उसके उसी ज़र्ब से मर जाए, इसमें क़सामत वाजिब होगी। मालिक ने कहा क़त्ल-ए-अम्द में एक शख्स आज़ाद के एवज़ में कई शख्स आज़ाद मारे जाएंगे जब सब क़त्ल में शरीक हों, इसी तरह औरतों और ग़ुलामों में भी हुक्म होगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">کہا مالک نے ایک شخص نے دوسرے کو لکڑی سے مار ڈالا عبدالملک بن مروان نے قاتل کو ولی مقتول کے حوالے کیا اس نے بھی اس کو لکڑی سے مار ڈالا۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اتفاقی ہے کہ اگر کوئی شخص کسی کو لکڑی یا پتھر سے قصداً مارے اور وہ ہلاک ہو جائے تو قصاص لیا جائے گا۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک قتل عمد یہی ہے کہ ایک آدمی دوسرے کو قصداً مارے یہاں تک کہ اس کا دم نکل جائے اور یہ بھی قتل عمد ہے کہ ایک شخص سے دشمنی ہو اس کو ایک ضرب لگا کر چلا آئے اس وقت وہ زندہ ہو بعد اس کے اسی ضرب سے مر جائے اس میں قسامت واجب ہوگی ۔ کہا مالک نے قتل عمد میں ایک شخص آزاد کے عوض میں کئی شخص آزاد مارے جائیں گے کہ جب سب قتل میں شریک ہوں اسی طرح عورتوں اور غلاموں میں بھی حکم ہوگا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik ne kaha ek shakhs ne dusre ko lakdi se maar dala, Abdul Malik bin Marwan ne qatil ko wali-e-maqtool ke hawale kiya, usne bhi usko lakdi se maar dala. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek yeh hukm ittefaqi hai ki agar koi shakhs kisi ko lakdi ya pathar se qasdan maare aur woh halak ho jaye to qisas liya jayega. Malik ne kaha hamare nazdeek qatl-e-amd yahi hai ki ek aadmi dusre ko qasdan maare yahan tak ki uska dam nikal jaye, aur yeh bhi qatl-e-amd hai ki ek shakhs se dushmani ho, usko ek zarb laga kar chala aaye, us waqt woh zinda ho, baad uske usi zarb se mar jaye, ismein qasamat wajib hogi. Malik ne kaha qatl-e-amd mein ek shakhs azaad ke ewaz mein kayi shakhs azaad maare jayenge jab sab qatl mein shareek hon, isi tarah auraton aur ghulamon mein bhi hukm hoga.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1592" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1573

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1593)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1593]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1593</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1593 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ، كَتَبَ إِلَى مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنِ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ يَذْكُرُ أَنَّهُ أُتِيَ بِسَكْرَانَ قَدْ قَتَلَ رَجُلاً فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ مُعَاوِيَةُ أَنِ اقْتُلْهُ بِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى قَالَ مَالِكٌ أَحْسَنُ مَا سَمِعْتُ فِي تَأْوِيلِ هَذِهِ الآيَةِ قَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى ‏{‏الْحُرُّ بِالْحُرِّ وَالْعَبْدُ بِالْعَبْدِ ‏}‏ فَهَؤُلاَءِ الذُّكُورُ ‏{‏وَالأُنْثَى بِالأُنْثَى‏}‏ أَنَّ الْقِصَاصَ يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الإِنَاثِ كَمَا يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الذُّكُورِ وَالْمَرْأَةُ الْحُرَّةُ تُقْتَلُ بِالْمَرْأَةِ الْحُرَّةِ كَمَا يُقْتَلُ الْحُرُّ بِالْحُرِّ وَالأَمَةُ تُقْتَلُ بِالأَمَةِ كَمَا يُقْتَلُ الْعَبْدُ بِالْعَبْدِ وَالْقِصَاصُ يَكُونُ بَيْنَ النِّسَاءِ كَمَا يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الرِّجَالِ وَالْقِصَاصُ أَيْضًا يَكُونُ بَيْنَ الرِّجَالِ وَالنِّسَاءِ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ اللَّهَ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى قَالَ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏وَكَتَبْنَا عَلَيْهِمْ فِيهَا أَنَّ النَّفْسَ بِالنَّفْسِ وَالْعَيْنَ بِالْعَيْنِ وَالأَنْفَ بِالأَنْفِ وَالأُذُنَ بِالأُذُنِ وَالسِّنَّ بِالسِّنِّ وَالْجُرُوحَ قِصَاصٌ ‏}‏ فَذَكَرَ اللَّهُ تَبَارَكَ وَتَعَالَى أَنَّ النَّفْسَ بِالنَّفْسِ فَنَفْسُ الْمَرْأَةِ الْحُرَّةِ بِنَفْسِ الرَّجُلِ الْحُرِّ وَجُرْحُهَا بِجُرْحِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يُمْسِكُ الرَّجُلَ لِلرَّجُلِ فَيَضْرِبُهُ فَيَمُوتُ مَكَانَهُ أَنَّهُ إِنْ أَمْسَكَهُ وَهُوَ يَرَى أَنَّهُ يُرِيدُ قَتْلَهُ قُتِلاَ بِهِ جَمِيعًا وَإِنْ أَمْسَكَهُ وَهُوَ يَرَى أَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ الضَّرْبَ مِمَّا يَضْرِبُ بِهِ النَّاسُ لاَ يَرَى أَنَّهُ عَمَدَ لِقَتْلِهِ فَإِنَّهُ يُقْتَلُ الْقَاتِلُ وَيُعَاقَبُ الْمُمْسِكُ أَشَدَّ الْعُقُوبَةِ وَيُسْجَنُ سَنَةً لأَنَّهُ أَمْسَكَهُ وَلاَ يَكُونُ عَلَيْهِ الْقَتْلُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَقْتُلُ الرَّجُلَ عَمْدًا أَوْ يَفْقَأُ عَيْنَهُ عَمْدًا فَيُقْتَلُ الْقَاتِلُ أَوْ تُفْقَأُ عَيْنُ الْفَاقِئِ</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan (companion). Select the transmitted exchange between Marwan and Muawiya, with the decision attributed to Muawiya. Malik&#x27;s following legal exposition and Quran interpretation remain source commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Marwan ibn al-Hakam wrote to Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan to mention to him that a drunkard was brought to him who had killed a man. Muawiya wrote to him to kill him in retaliation for the dead man.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1593]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1593</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1593 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;The best of what I have heard on the interpretation of this ayat, the word of Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, &#x27;The free man for the free man and the slave for the slave - these are men and the woman for the woman,&#x27; (Sura 2 ayat 178) is that retaliation is between women as it is between men. The free woman is killed for the free woman as the free man is killed for the free man. The slave-girl is slain for the slave-girl as the slave is slain for the slave. Retaliation is between women as it is between men. That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;We have written for them in it that it is a life for a life and an eye for an eye, a nose for a nose, and an ear for an ear, and a tooth for a tooth, and for wounds there is retaliation.&#x27; (Sura 5 ayat 48) Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, mentioned that it is a life for a life. It is the life of a free woman for the life of a free man, and her injury for his injury.&quot; Malik said about a man who held a man fast for another man to hit, and he died on the spot, &quot;If he held him and he thought that he meant to kill him, the two of them are both killed for him. If he held him and he thought that he meant to beat him as people sometimes do, and he did not think that he meant to kill him, the murderer is slain and the one who held him is punished with a very severe punishment and jailed for a year. There is no killing against him.&quot; Malik said about a man who murdered a man intentionally or gouged out his eye intentionally, and then was slain or had his eye gouged out himself before retaliation was inflicted on him, &quot;There is no blood-money nor retaliation against him. The right of the one who was killed or had his eye gouged out goes when the thing which he is claiming as retaliation goes. It is the same with a man who murders another man intentionally and then the murderer dies. When the murderer dies, the one seeking blood-revenge has nothing of blood- money or anything else. That is by the word of Allah, the Blessed the Exalted, &#x27;Retaliation is written for you in killing. The free man for the free man and the slave for the slave.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;He only has retaliation against the one who killed him. If the man who murdered him dies, he has no retaliation or blood-money.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no retaliation held against a free man by a slave for any injury. The slave is killed for the free man when he intentionally murders him. The free man is not slain for the slave, even if he murders him intentionally. It is the best of what I have heard</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1593]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1593</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1593 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) को पहुंचा की मरवान बिन हकम ने मुआवियाह बिन अबी सुफ़यान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) को लिखा की एक शख्स ने नशे की हालत में एक शख्स को मार डाला, मुआवियाह ने जवाब लिखा की तू भी उसको मार डाल। मालिक ने कहा मैंने इसकी तफ़सीर बहुत अच्छी सुनी, फ़रमाया अल्लाह तआला ने क़त्ल कर आज़ाद को आज़ाद के बदले में और ग़ुलाम को ग़ुलाम के बदले में और औरत को औरत के बदले में, तो क़िसास औरतों में आपस में लिया जाएगा जैसा की मर्दों में लिया जाता है, और मर्द और औरत में भी लिया जाएगा क्योंकि अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहू फ़रमाता है नफ़्स बदले नफ़्स के क़त्ल किया जाएगा, तो औरत मर्द के बदले में क़त्ल की जाएगी और मर्द औरत के बदले में मारा जाएगा, इसी तरह एक दूसरे को अगर ज़ख्मी करेगा तब भी क़िसास लिया जाएगा। मालिक ने कहा अगर एक शख्स एक शख्स को पकड़ ले और दूसरा उसको आकर मार डाले और मालूम हो जाए की उसने मार डालने ही के वास्ते पकड़ा था तो दोनों शख्स उसके बदले में क़त्ल किए जाएंगे, अगर उसने इस नियत से नहीं पकड़ा था बल्कि उसको यह ख़याल था की दूसरा शख्स यूंही उसे मारेगा तो पकड़ने वाला क़त्ल न किया जाएगा लेकिन उसको सख्त सज़ा दी जाएगी और बाद सज़ा के एक बरस तक क़ैद किया जाएगा। मालिक ने कहा ज़ैद ने अम्र को क़त्ल किया या उसकी आंख फोड़ डाली, क़स्दन, अब क़ब्ल उसके की ज़ैद से क़िसास लिया जाए उसको बक्र ने मार डाला या ज़ैद की आंख फोड़ डाली तो उस पर दियत या क़िसास वाजिब न होगा क्योंकि अम्र का हक़ ज़ैद की जान में था या उसकी आंख में, अब ज़ैद ही न रहा या वो आंख ही न रही। इसकी नज़ीर यह है की ज़ैद अम्र को अम्दन मार डाले, फिर ज़ैद भी मर जाए तो अम्र के वारिसों को अब कुछ न मिलेगा क्योंकि क़िसास कातिल पर होता है जब वो खुद मर गया तो न क़िसास है न दियत। मालिक ने कहा आज़ाद और ग़ुलाम में क़िसास नहीं है ज़ख्मों में, लेकिन अगर ग़ुलाम आज़ाद को मार डालेगा तो ग़ुलाम मारा जाएगा और जो आज़ाद ग़ुलाम को मार डालेगा तो आज़ाद न मारा जाएगा, यह मैंने बहुत अच्छी सुनी।</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ مروان بن حکم نے معاویہ بن ابی سفیان کو لکھا کہ ایک شخص نے نشے کی حالت میں ایک شخص کو مار ڈالا معاویہ نے جواب لکھا کہ تو بھی اس کو مار ڈال۔ کہا مالک نے میں نے اس کی تفسیر بہت اچھی سنی فرمایا اللہ تعالیٰ نے قتل کر آزاد کو آزاد کے بدلے میں اور غلام کو غلام کے بدلے میں اور عورت کو عورت کے بدلے میں تو قصاص عورتوں میں آپس میں لیا جائے گا جیسا کہ مردوں میں لیا جاتا ہے اور مرد اور عورت میں بھی لیا جائے گا کیونکہ اللہ جل جلالہ فرماتا ہے نفس بدلے نفس کے قتل کیا جائے گا تو عورت مرد کے بدلے میں قتل کی جائے گی اور مرد عورت کے بدلے میں مارا جائے گا اسی طرح ایک دوسرے کو اگر زخمی کرے گا تب بھی قصاص لیا جائے گا۔ کہا مالک نے اگر ایک شخص ایک شخص کو پکڑ لے اور دوسرا اس کو آکر مار ڈالے اور معلوم ہو جائے کہ اس نے مار ڈالنے ہی کے واسطے پکڑا تھا تو دونوں شخص اس کے بدلے میں قتل کئے جائیں گے اگر اس نے اس نیت سے نہیں پکڑا تھا بلکہ اس کو یہ خیال تھا کہ دوسرا شخص یوں ہی اسے مارے گا تو پکڑنے والا قتل نہ کیا جائے گا لیکن اس کو سخت سزادی جائے گی۔ اور بعد سزا کے ایک برس تک قید کیا جائے گا۔ کہا مالک نے زید نے عمرو کو قتل کیا یا اس کی آنکھ پھوڑ ڈالی ، قصداً اب قبل اس کے کہ زید سے قصاص لیا جائے اس کو بکر نے مار ڈالا یا زید کی آنکھ پھوڑ ڈالی تو اس پر دیت یا قصاص واجب نہ ہوگا کیونکہ عمرو کا حق زید کی جان میں تھا یا اس کی آنکھ میں اب زید ہی نہ رہا یا وہ آنکھ ہی نہ رہی۔ اس کی نظیر یہ ہے کہ زید عمرو کو عمداً مار ڈالے گا پھر زید بھی مرجائے تو عمرو کے وارثوں کو اب کچھ نہ ملے گا کیونکہ قصاص قاتل پر ہوتا ہے جب وہ خود مرگیا تو نہ قصاص ہے نہ دیت۔ کہا مالک نے آزاد اور غلام میں قصاص نہیں ہے زخموں میں لیکن اگر غلام آزاد کو مار ڈالے گا تو غلام مارا جائے گا اور جو آزاد غلام کو مار ڈالے گا تو آزاد نہ مارا جائے گا یہ میں نے بہت اچھا سنا۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1593]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1593</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1593 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik (Rahmatullah Alaih) ko pahuncha ki Marwan bin Hakam ne Muawiyah bin Abi Sufyan (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko likha ki ek shakhs ne nashe ki halat mein ek shakhs ko maar dala, Muawiyah ne jawab likha ki tu bhi usko maar daal. Malik ne kaha maine iski tafseer bahut achi suni, farmaya Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne qatl kar azaad ko azaad ke badle mein aur ghulam ko ghulam ke badle mein aur aurat ko aurat ke badle mein, to qisas auraton mein aapas mein liya jayega jaisa ki mardon mein liya jata hai, aur mard aur aurat mein bhi liya jayega kyunki Allah Jalla Jalaluhu farmata hai nafs badle nafs ke qatl kiya jayega, to aurat mard ke badle mein qatl ki jayegi aur mard aurat ke badle mein mara jayega, isi tarah ek dusre ko agar zakhmi karega tab bhi qisas liya jayega. Malik ne kaha agar ek shakhs ek shakhs ko pakad le aur dusra usko aakar maar dale aur maloom ho jaye ki usne maar dalne hi ke waste pakda tha to dono shakhs uske badle mein qatl kiye jayenge, agar usne is niyat se nahi pakda tha balki usko yeh khayal tha ki dusra shakhs yunhi use maarega to pakadne wala qatl na kiya jayega lekin usko sakht saza di jayegi aur baad saza ke ek baras tak qaid kiya jayega. Malik ne kaha Zaid ne Amr ko qatl kiya ya uski aankh phod dali, qasdan, ab qabl uske ki Zaid se qisas liya jaye usko Bakr ne maar dala ya Zaid ki aankh phod dali to us par diyat ya qisas wajib na hoga kyunki Amr ka haq Zaid ki jaan mein tha ya uski aankh mein, ab Zaid hi na raha ya woh aankh hi na rahi. Iski nazeer yeh hai ki Zaid Amr ko amdan maar dale, phir Zaid bhi mar jaye to Amr ke warison ko ab kuch na milega kyunki qisas qatil par hota hai jab woh khud mar gaya to na qisas hai na diyat. Malik ne kaha azaad aur ghulam mein qisas nahi hai zakhmon mein, lekin agar ghulam azaad ko maar dalega to ghulam mara jayega aur jo azaad ghulam ko maar dalega to azaad na mara jayega, yeh maine bahut achi suni.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1593" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1574

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1594)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 43</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ (successor). The English and Arabic fields were read independently and contain different reports under this provider number. English transmits an unnamed knowledgeable person&#x27;s view; Arabic reports Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm&#x27;s decision. No translation equivalence is claimed; English compiler commentary stays separate.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَقَادَ مِنْ كَسْرِ الْفَخِذِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1594]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1594" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The English and Arabic fields were read independently and contain different reports under this provider number. English transmits an unnamed knowledgeable person&#x27;s view; Arabic reports Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm&#x27;s decision. No translation equivalence is claimed; English compiler commentary stays separate.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he saw whomever he was satisfied with among the people of knowledge say about a man who willed that his murderer be pardoned when he murdered him intentionally, &quot;That is permitted for him. He is more entitled to the man&#x27;s blood than any of his relatives after him.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1594]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1594" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1594</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1594 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said about a man who pardoned murder, after he had claimed his right and it was obliged for him, &quot;There is no blood-money against the murderer unless the one who pardons him stipulates that when he pardons him.&quot; Malik said about the murderer when he was pardoned, &quot;He is flogged one hundred lashes and jailed for a year.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a man murders intentionally and there is a clear proof of that, and the murdered man has sons and daughters and the sons pardon and the daughters refuse to pardon, the pardon of the sons is permitted in opposition to the daughters and there is no authority for the daughters with the sons in demanding blood and pardoning</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1594" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मालिक से रिवायत किया कि उन्होंने अहल-इ-इल्म में से जिन्हें वह पसंद करते थे उन्हें यह कहते सुना कि जो शख्स इरादा करे के उसका कातिल माफ़ कर दिया जाये जब उसने उसे जान बूझ कर क़त्ल किया हो तो यह उसके लिए जायज़ है. वह उसके खून का अपने बाद बाकी रिश्तेदारों से ज़्यादा हक़दार है.</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ سَائِبَةً، أَعْتَقَهُ بَعْضُ الْحُجَّاجِ فَقَتَلَ ابْنَ رَجُلٍ مِنْ بَنِي عَائِذٍ فَجَاءَ الْعَائِذِيُّ أَبُو الْمَقْتُولِ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ يَطْلُبُ دِيَةَ ابْنِهِ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ لاَ دِيَةَ لَهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الْعَائِذِيُّ أَرَأَيْتَ لَوْ قَتَلَهُ ابْنِي فَقَالَ عُمَرُ إِذًا تُخْرِجُونَ دِيَتَهُ فَقَالَ هُوَ إِذًا كَالأَرْقَمِ إِنْ يُتْرَكْ يَلْقَمْ وَإِنْ يُقْتَلْ يَنْقَمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1595" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Abu Bakr ibn Muhammd ibn Amr ibn Hazm (successor). The complete English field contains two transmitted reports after Malik&#x27;s legal exposition. Preserve Abu Bakr ibn Muhammd&#x27;s decision and the separately transmitted Umar exchange as distinct reports; the opening compiler exposition remains commentary. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The complete English field contains two transmitted reports after Malik&#x27;s legal exposition. Preserve Abu Bakr ibn Muhammd&#x27;s decision and the separately transmitted Umar exchange as distinct reports; the opening compiler exposition remains commentary.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Abu Bakr ibn Muhammd ibn Amr ibn Hazm took retaliation for the breaking of a leg.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from Sulayman ibn Yasar that a slave was set free by one of the people on hajj and his master had abandoned the right to inherit from him. The ex-slave then killed a man from the Banu A&#x27;idh tribe. An A&#x27;idhi, the father of the slain man came to Umar ibn al-Khattab seeking the blood-money of his son. Umar said, &quot;He has no blood-money.&quot; The A&#x27;idhi said, &quot;What would you think if it had been my son who killed him?&quot; Umar said, &quot;Then you would pay his blood-money.&quot; He said, &quot;He is then like the black and white Arqam snake. If it is left, it devours and if it is killed, it takes revenge</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1595]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1595" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1595</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1595 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya said that Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things in our community is that retaliation is taken from someone who breaks someone&#x27;s hand or foot intentionally and not blood-money.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Retaliation is not inflicted on anyone until the wound of the injured party has healed. Then retaliation is inflicted on him. If the wound of the person on whom the retaliation has been inflicted is like the first person&#x27;s wound when it heals, it is retaliation. If the wound of the one on whom the retaliation has been inflicted becomes worse or he dies, there is nothing held against the one who has taken retaliation. If the wound of the person on whom the retaliation has been inflicted heals and the injured party is paralysed or his injury has healed but he has a scar, defect, or blemish, the person on whom the retaliation has been inflicted does not have his hand broken again and further retaliation is not taken for his injury.&quot; He said, &quot;But there is blood-money from him according to what he has impaired or maimed of the hand of the injured party. The bodily injury is also like that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When a man intentionally goes to his wife and gouges out her eye or breaks her hand or cuts off her finger or such like, and does it intentionally, retaliation is inflicted on him. As for a man who strikes his wife with a rope or a whip and hits what he did not mean to hit or does what he did not intend to do, he pays blood-money for what he has struck according to this principle, and retaliation is not inflicted on him.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1595" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> </p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसर से रिवायत है की एक सा&#x27;इबा (आज़ाद करदा लौंडी) ने जिसको किसी हाजी ने आज़ाद कर दिया था के बेटे को जो बानी आज़ में था मार डाला. मक़तूल का बाप हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास अपने बेटे की दियत मांगने आया. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा इसके लिए दियत नहीं है. वह शख्स बोला अगर मेरा बेटा सा&#x27;इबा को मार डालता तो तुम क्या हुक्म करते? हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा कि उस वक़्त तुमको उसकी दियत ऐडा करनी होती. वह शख्स बोला फिर तो सा&#x27;इबा क्या है? एक चितला सांप है</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1595" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ ایک سائبہ نے جس کو کسی حاجی نے آزاد کر دیا تھا ایک شخص کے بیٹے کو جو بنی عاذ میں تھا مار ڈالا مقتول کا باپ حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس اپنے بیٹے کی دیت مانگنے آیا حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا اس کے لئے دیت نہیں ہے وہ شخص بولا اگر میرا بیٹا سائبہ کو مار ڈالتا تو تم کیا حکم کرتے حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا کہ اس وقت تم کو اس کی دیت ادا کرنی ہوتی وہ شخص بولا پھر تو سائبہ کیا ہے ایک چتلا سانپ ہے اگر چھوڑ دو تو ڈس لے اگر مارو تو بدلہ لے ۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1595 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ki ek Sa&#x27;iba (azaad karda laundi) ne jisko kisi haaji ne azaad kar diya tha, ek shakhs ke bete ko jo Bani Aaz mein tha maar dala. Maqtool ka baap Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas apne bete ki diyat maangne aaya. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha iske liye diyat nahi hai. Woh shakhs bola agar mera beta Sa&#x27;iba ko maar dalta to tum kya hukm karte? Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha ki us waqt tumko uski diyat ada karni hoti. Woh shakhs bola phir to Sa&#x27;iba kya hai? Ek chitla saanp hai, agar chhod do to das le, agar maaro to badla le.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1595" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
